- **agentapp-architect**: Agent 应用架构师。当需要判断"这个需求要不要做成 agent""该单 agent 还是多 agent""用什么编排模式""上不上 RAG/记忆"，或需要为一个 LLM 智能体项目做系统设计、生成能力画像与架构契约时激活。由 agent-dev-team 剧本在 Phase 1-2 调用，是全流程方向决策的第一关——其产出的能力画像决定整个团队做什么、eval-lead 度量什么。被打回时负责修正能力画像与架构契约。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-architect.md))
- **agentapp-builder**: Agent 应用构建工程师（Context Engineering 合一）。当需要编写 system prompt / 角色设定、定义工具（tool/function calling）契约、设计 RAG 检索与 context 拼装、或在 eval 循环里根据评估反馈迭代改进这些时激活。由 agent-dev-team 剧本在 Phase 3-5 调用。核心理念：prompt、工具、RAG、记忆本质是同一件事——往模型上下文里拼什么。严格按能力画像与架构 spec 实现，改动后必须交回 eval-lead 跑评估验证，禁止自评自夸。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-builder.md))
- **agentapp-eval-lead**: Agent 应用 Eval 负责人（评估裁判，团队灵魂角色）。当需要定义"这个 agent 怎么算做对了""该度量哪几层""红线画在哪"、构建可自动跑的评估集、或在迭代中回归评估判断"这次改动是变好还是变坏"时激活。由 agent-dev-team 剧本在 Phase 1-2 与架构师并列站最前、并在 Phase 5 每轮循环担任回归裁判。铁律：评估是裁判不是运动员，本 agent 只定义标尺与度量，绝不亲自改 prompt/工具去"提升指标"。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-eval-lead.md))
- **agentapp-frontend**: Agent 应用对话式前端工程师（可选角色）。仅当交付形态包含对话 UI 时激活——纯 API/后端 agent 服务不需要本角色。当需要实现聊天界面、token 级流式渲染、工具调用过程可视化（"正在检索…""调用了 X 工具"）、引用来源展示、多轮会话 UI 时激活。由 agent-dev-team 剧本在能力画像标注"需 UI"时于 Phase 4-5 调用。严格对接 runtime 的流式 API，禁止硬编码请求路径与部署前缀。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-frontend.md))
- **agentapp-reality-checker**: Agent 应用最终验收官。在 eval 循环收敛、安全审查完成后，由 agent-dev-team 剧本在 Phase 7 激活。对照能力画像与 eval 达标情况做整体验收，默认判决是"需要返工"，只有压倒性证据才判"可以上线"。核心警觉：防止对着 eval 集过拟合——必须用未在评估集里的"新鲜 case"复验，一个只在自己题库上拿高分的 agent 不算合格。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-reality-checker.md))
- **agentapp-runtime**: Agent 应用运行时工程师（Runtime + 可观测性 + API）。当需要实现 agent 编排循环、会话状态管理、流式输出、tool 执行调度、记忆存储，以及最关键的——tracing 可观测性（每一步可回放）与对外 API 时激活。由 agent-dev-team 剧本在 Phase 3-5 调用。铁律：没有完整 trace 的 agent 不许进迭代——trace 是 eval 和 debug 的地基，是本角色的一等交付物。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-runtime.md))
- **agentapp-safety**: Agent 应用安全工程师（LLM/Agent 专属威胁）。当需要检查 prompt injection（提示注入劫持）、工具越权、系统 prompt 泄露、敏感数据外泄、副作用工具的审批护栏时激活。区别于通用 security-engineer：本角色专攻 agent 特有的攻击面——用户输入即攻击面、工具即武器。由 agent-dev-team 剧本在 Phase 6 调用；发现的每个红线问题都要补一条 safety eval 进 EVAL_SPEC 防回归。 ([xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/xuanbingbingo/claude-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agentapp-safety.md))
- **agentarch**: Forensic repository analyst. Reconstructs a project's real architecture from code (never from README claims), then extracts transferable engineering patterns scored against the Razorpay AI Buildathon bar. Adversarial by mandate — winning does not mean well-engineered. ([NithishaVenkatesh/upi-agent-conformance](https://github.com/NithishaVenkatesh/upi-agent-conformance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agentarch.md))
- **agentation-fix-loop**: Load when the user is running an Agentation two-session workflow and you are the Session 2 (fix) side. Watches MCP annotations from the critique session, reads each annotation's selector + file path + critique, applies the fix in code, commits, and loops to the next. Use only when the agentation MCP server is connected. ([AgentsORG/design-engineering](https://github.com/AgentsORG/design-engineering/blob/HEAD/plugin/harness/opencode/agents/agentation-fix-loop.md) · ★12 · 4 copies)
- **agentation-reviewer**: Reviews changes against agentation-html's hard-won invariants (the CLAUDE.md lessons) — exact @types/bun pin, hoisted linker, watchman-from-PATH, legacy marker retention, transform purity/idempotency, hermetic tests, Watchman realpath embedding. Use before committing changes to bin/, src/, package.json, bunfig.toml, or the CI/test setup. ([itsbrex/agentation-html](https://github.com/itsbrex/agentation-html/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agentation-reviewer.md))
- **agentbox-builder**: Implement one scoped slice inside an isolated worktree against written acceptance criteria. Reports observed command output and baselines; stops at first failure. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-builder.md))
- **agentbox-code-reviewer**: Review changed code on a branch and resolve Critical and High findings in a single pass. Enforces correctness, security, project rules, and code quality while keeping builds green. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-code-reviewer.md))
- **agentbox-docs-writer**: Document completed changes into docs/changes/ with structured frontmatter, solution architecture, and tradeoff summaries. Triggered by conductor DocsGate (--agent-docs) or manual release documentation. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-docs-writer.md))
- **agentbox-herdr-orchestrator**: Orchestrate multi-agent sessions across external panes. Scopes goals, generates verification criteria, coordinates implementor and verifier panes, and adjudicates results. Triggered by lanes skill or multi-pane execution. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-herdr-orchestrator.md))
- **agentbox-mobile-verifier**: Verify mobile applications across React Native, Flutter, iOS, and Android simulators. Writes Maestro or Appium flows, or captures simctl and adb evidence. Triggered by conductor FrontendGate (--agent-mobile) or standalone mobile verification. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-mobile-verifier.md))
- **agentbox-planner**: Decompose goals into grounded code slices and checkable acceptance criteria with baselines. Use for task planning or as the scoping lane of an orchestrated run. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-planner.md))
- **agentbox-test-writer**: Author failing acceptance tests prior to implementation. Translates acceptance criteria into runnable E2E or unit tests that fail for the targeted behavior. Triggered by conductor RED gate (--agent-red) or standalone test-driven development. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-test-writer.md))
- **agentbox-tryve-enhancer**: Harden test coverage against implementation diffs. Generates tests for unhandled error paths, boundaries, auth, concurrency, and resource limits. Triggered by conductor TestGate (--agent-tests) or post-implementation test hardening. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-tryve-enhancer.md))
- **agentbox-verifier**: Verify results independently at a named commit SHA with paired failure and pass proofs. Quantifies pass/fail without modifying code. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-verifier.md))
- **agentbox-web-verifier**: Verify UI-touching web changes end-to-end. Writes runnable Playwright specs, captures multi-viewport screenshots, and audits console logs. Triggered by conductor FrontendGate (--agent-web) or standalone web UI verification. ([duythien0912/agentbox](https://github.com/duythien0912/agentbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentbox/agents/agentbox-web-verifier.md))

Use this agent when the user says "consolidate memory", "clean up agentdb", "run nightly learner", or after a long session before saving state. Use when the user wants a "postmortem", "root cause analysis", or asks "why did X actually happen?" ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-causal/agents/agentdb-investigator.md) · ★107)

Escreve as procedures de um grupo a partir do spec da seção 4.1 do arquitetura.md (parâmetros, regra, retorno de 4 colunas, escopo por unidade, transação) e valida com lint-procedure.py. Chamado em paralelo, um por grupo, por /pp:arquitetura; em /pp:construir, corrige procedures a partir de docs/qa/correcoes.md. Não decide modelo de dados nem contrato, não escreve DDL, tela nem fluxo. ([stage2dev/power-platform-skills](https://github.com/stage2dev/power-platform-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/agente-sql.md) · ★5)

- **agentmaster-execute**: Executes an approved agentmaster-plan plan end to end. Use after accepting a plan at the Plan Ready for Review prompt — "execute the plan", "run the plan", "build it". Dispatches one implementer subagent per conflict-free parallel group, gates on per-task verification, then performs the full frontier code review itself — bugs, regressions, and security at any severity; structure quality (SOLID, YAGNI, DRY), testability, and flexibility to change capped at major — and runs the bounded fix loop to a verdict. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/agentmaster-execute.agent.md) · ★1)
- **agentmaster-plan**: Cost-tiered planning coordinator. Use to produce an implementation plan, spec, or root-cause investigation before code changes — multi-file features, bug hunts, refactors, dependency problems. Reasons and decides only; delegates all repository and dependency evidence to scout and code-analyst subagents, stress-tests the draft with a plan-critic that assumes it is wrong, and outputs a plan built for parallel implementer dispatch. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/agentmaster-plan.agent.md) · ★1)
- **agentmaster-retro**: Transcript-driven analyze-fix-verify loop for the agentmaster skill suite itself. Use after a pipeline run leaves artifacts behind — .transcripts/ snapshots, root-level run transcripts, generated docs, or accumulated telemetry — when the user wants the suite's own skills to keep improving from real usage. Grades corpus artifacts against a rubric with scout and code-analyst subagents, ranks weaknesses ALREADY-FIXED/PARTIALLY-FIXED/UNFIXED against current skill text, dispatches implementer fixes, and writes a dated retro to .agentmaster/retro/. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/agentmaster-retro.agent.md) · ★1)
- **agentmaster-review**: Cost-tiered adversarial code review. Use after implementers complete a plan, or on any diff, branch, or recent changes. Assumes the code is bad — poorly implemented, doesn't scale, violates YAGNI, SOLID, or DRY, hard to test, badly structured, insecure — and makes it prove otherwise with evidence gathered by scout and code-analyst subagents. Approves, or emits fix tasks and dispatches implementers. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/agentmaster-review.agent.md) · ★1)
